49: // All DB keys that should exist: current FY + previous FY + any FY with data + next FY
50: // v1.4.2 — repair legacy Sale-N return attribution.
51: //
52: // Older builds could leave two different completed resale returns tagged to the
53: // same sale number (most commonly Sale 1). That makes the P&L engine do exactly
54: // what the bad data says: charge multiple full refunds against one sale and can
55: // turn a small fulfilment loss into a huge fictional lifetime loss.
56: //
57: // Full returns already contain an immutable `_dateSoldAtReturn` snapshot. When
58: // there are 2+ full returns with distinct sold dates, chronological sold dates
59: // are authoritative: first completed sale = Sale 1, next = Sale 2, etc. This is
60: // safer than refund/log dates because a historical return can be entered later.
61: // Same-day/ambiguous records are deliberately NOT auto-renumbered.
62: function _returnSaleSnapshotDate(r){
63:   const d=r&&r._dateSoldAtReturn;
64:   return d?String(d).slice(0,10):null;
65: }

118: // Launch migration entry point. First repair reliable full-return sequences,
119: // then retain the old conservative fallback for any genuinely untagged rows
120: // whose historical snapshots are too old to reconstruct safely.
121: function _migrateSaleNoTags(){
122:   let touched=0;
123:   try{
124:     (typeof allDBKeys==='function'?allDBKeys():[]).forEach(function(k){
125:       (Array.isArray(DB[k])?DB[k]:[]).forEach(function(it){
126:         if(!it||!Array.isArray(it.returnHistory)||!it.returnHistory.length)return;
127:         let itemTouched=_repairSaleNoSequenceForItem(it);
128:         const rd=it.resaleDateSold?String(it.resaleDateSold).slice(0,10):null;
129:         const fullNos=it.returnHistory.filter(function(r){return r&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r.saleNo!=null;})
130:           .map(function(r){return Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);});
131:         const maxFullNo=fullNos.length?Math.max.apply(null,fullNos):1;
132:         // If the item is currently fully returned, the latest full-return row
133:         // already represents the current sale. Otherwise the live resale (if
134:         // any) is the cycle after the latest completed full return.
135:         const currentResaleNo=(it.isReturned&&maxFullNo>=2)?maxFullNo:Math.max(2,maxFullNo+1);
136:         it.returnHistory.forEach(function(r){
137:           if(!r||r.saleNo!=null)return;
138:           const logged=r.loggedAt?String(r.loggedAt).slice(0,10):null;
139:           if(rd&&logged&&logged>=rd){r.saleNo=currentResaleNo;itemTouched++;}
140:           else {r.saleNo=1;itemTouched++;}
141:         });
142:         if(itemTouched){
143:           touched+=itemTouched;
144:           try{if(typeof calcGrossProfit==='function')it.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(it);}catch(e){}
145:         }
146:       });
147:     });
148:   }catch(e){console.warn('[RETRADE] Sale-N history repair failed',e);}
149:   return touched;
150: }

182: // Guarantees DB has the expected shape: an array for every allDBKeys() month,
183: // plus DB.trips and DB.expenses. Idempotent — safe to call anywhere. Fixes
184: // the "DB[m] is undefined" crash for brand-new users whose loadFromSupabase
185: // returned zero rows and therefore never populated any month keys.
186: function _ensureDBShape(){
187:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
188:     if(!Array.isArray(DB[k])) DB[k]=[];
189:   });
190:   if(!Array.isArray(DB.trips)) DB.trips=[];
191:   if(!Array.isArray(DB.expenses)) DB.expenses=[];
192: }

194: // Patch A — Item state migration. One-time pass: every item must have a
195: // canonical `state` field of 'sourced' | 'listed' | 'sold' | 'returned'.
196: // State is purely for filtering/display in this patch — financial logic
197: // continues to read existing fields (sold/isReturned/resaleSalePrice etc).
198: // Derivation rules (in order):
199: //   active return in returnHistory[] (no resale yet)         → 'returned'
200: //   has dateSold or resaleSalePrice (and not returned)       → 'sold'
201: //   otherwise                                                → 'listed'
202: // Stock items (state='stock') are pre-listing and will only ever exist if
203: // they were created via the Stock Quick Add flow — never produced by this
204: // migration (no existing field can imply pre-listing intent).
205: //
206: // ALSO: backfill dateSourced for items that don't have it. Anything created
207: // via the old List Quick Add flow gets dateSourced=dateListed (effectively
208: // "sourced and listed same day" → 0d time-to-list). Falls back to dateSold
209: // for ancient entries with neither, ensuring the field is never undefined.
210: // This matters for the Stock page's "Avg time to list" KPI which would
211: // otherwise have to skip un-sourced items entirely.
212: function _migrateItemStates(){
213:   const _VALID_STATES=['sourced','listed','sold','returned'];
214:   let touchedState=0, touchedSourced=0, reconciled=0;
215:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
216:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
217:       // E7: remap legacy 'stock' → 'sourced'. Items saved before E6 renamed the
218:       // pre-listing state carry 'stock', which the DB CHECK constraint rejects.
219:       // 'stock' is truthy so the !i.state guard below would silently skip it.
220:       if(i.state==='stock'){
221:         i.state='sourced';
222:         touchedState++;
223:       }
224:       // Catch null/undefined/empty/any other unrecognised value
225:       if(!i.state || !_VALID_STATES.includes(i.state)){
226:         const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
227:         const _activeReturn=i.isReturned===true || (_hadFullReturn && !i.resaleSalePrice);
228:         if(_activeReturn){ i.state='returned'; }
229:         else if(i.dateSold || i.resaleSalePrice){ i.state='sold'; }
230:         else { i.state='listed'; }
231:         touchedState++;
232:       }
233:       // v2.09.4 — Reconcile ghost states left by pre-v2.09.2 duplicates. An item
234:       // with state='sold' but no dateSold AND no resaleSalePrice AND not
235:       // returned isn't actually sold — the source item was, the duplicate
236:       // inherited the state but had its sale fields cleared. Stock Room shows
237:       // these correctly as listed (its filter ignores state), but the account
238:       // page and other state-driven UIs read the wrong badge. Correct here.
239:       // Same pattern for state='returned' without an isReturned/return record.
240:       if(i.state==='sold' && !i.dateSold && !i.resaleSalePrice && !i.isReturned){
241:         i.state='listed';
242:         reconciled++;
243:       } else if(i.state==='returned' && !i.isReturned){
244:         const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
245:         if(!_hadFullReturn){
246:           // No supporting evidence — treat as active. If dateSold survived,
247:           // it was truly sold before the return got cleared; otherwise listed.
248:           i.state=(i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)?'sold':'listed';
249:           reconciled++;
250:         }
251:       }
252:       if(!i.dateSourced){
253:         i.dateSourced = i.dateListed || i.dateSold || null;
254:         if(i.dateSourced) touchedSourced++;
255:       }
256:     });
257:   });
258:   if(reconciled>0)console.info('[RETRADE] v2.09.4 state reconcile: fixed '+reconciled+' ghost-sold/returned item'+(reconciled!==1?'s':''));
259:   return reconciled;
260: }

695: // Returns the fee for a platform id, or 0. Safe on unknown/null input.
696: function _listingFeeFor(platformId){
697:   return LISTING_FEES[platformId]||0;
698: }
699: // Stamps the sale-1 listing fee onto a NEWLY CREATED listing. Call this at the
700: // exact point an item becomes a live listing (creation, sourced→listed,
701: // duplicate) — never from a migration, a reconcile, or an undo, all of which
702: // would bill a listing that was never actually created.
703: function _stampListingFee(item){
704:   if(!item)return item;
705:   item.listingFee=_listingFeeFor(_s1Platform(item)); // v2.19.33 — Sale-1 platform
706:   return item;
707: }
708: // Stamps the sale-2 (relist) fee. A relist is a second, separately-billed
709: // listing on the SAME item record, which is why it gets its own field rather
710: // than accumulating into listingFee — mirroring resalePostage /
711: // resaleShippingCost / resalePromoPercent, which exist for the same reason.
712: // Sale 1's P&L keeps its own 36p; the relist P&L carries its own; the item
713: // totals 72p. See _saleBreakdown + calcGrossProfit.
714: function _stampRelistFee(item){
715:   if(!item)return item;
716:   // `resaleListingFee` is the LIVE/current resale-cycle fee only. Older relist
717:   // fees are frozen on the preceding full-return row (`_listingFeeAtRelist`).
718:   // Accumulating here made Sale 3 inherit Sale 2's insertion fee and broke both
719:   // correction paths and per-cycle reporting.
720:   item.resaleListingFee=_listingFeeFor(item.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(item));
721:   return item;
722: }

3156: // Fills the snapshot on legacy return entries created before the column existed.
3157: // Strategy per item:
3158: //   1. If r._dateSoldAtReturn already set → skip
3159: //   2. Else if i.dateSold present → use that (most cases)
3160: //   3. Else (full return wiped dateSold) → use the FULL return's loggedAt as
3161: //      last-resort approximation. The original sale was at-or-before the return
3162: //      logged date; for backdating purposes loggedAt is the best available proxy.
3163: // Only persists if something changed, so subsequent loads are zero-write.
3164: async function _backfillDateSoldAtReturn(){
3165:   // One-shot guard: once every return entry in this account has _dateSoldAtReturn
3166:   // set, we write a localStorage flag so subsequent loads skip the scan entirely.
3167:   // Key is user-agnostic (preview mode also benefits) — safe to share per-browser.
3168:   const _BACKFILL_KEY='retrade_backfill_dsar_v1';
3169:   if(localStorage.getItem(_BACKFILL_KEY)==='1')return;
3170:   let dirty=0;
3171:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
3172:     if(k==='trips'||k==='expenses'||k==='_userOwned'||k==='_taxYear')return;
3173:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
3174:       const rh=i.returnHistory||[];
3175:       if(rh.length===0)return;
3176:       const fullEntry=rh.find(r=>r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
3177:       const fallbackForFullReturn=fullEntry?(fullEntry.loggedAt||null):null;
3178:       rh.forEach(function(r){
3179:         if(r._dateSoldAtReturn)return; // already set, skip
3180:         const filled=i.dateSold||fallbackForFullReturn||null;
3181:         if(filled){
3182:           r._dateSoldAtReturn=filled;
3183:           dirty++;
3184:         }
3185:       });
3186:     });
3187:   });
3188:   if(dirty>0){
3189:     try{
3190:       // Re-save every item that has returnHistory so the column gets pushed up
3191:       // for ALL of them in a single sync. saveDB's debounced diff engine will
3192:       // batch this into one Supabase round trip.
3193:       await saveDB();
3194:     }catch(e){
3195:       console.warn('[backfill] save failed — values are correct in memory but not persisted:',e);
3196:     }
3197:   }
3198:   // Mark done regardless of whether anything was filled — if dirty=0 it means
3199:   // all entries were already set, so future loads can skip immediately.
3200:   try{ localStorage.setItem(_BACKFILL_KEY,'1'); }catch(e){}
3201: }

3203: // ── Missing-column resilience (v2.19.38) ────────────────────────────────────
3204: // A column the current app writes but the DB hasn't migrated yet (sale_platform,
3205: // resale_listing_fee, the *_at_relist return snapshots, sourcing_runs.ended_at_iso,
3206: // …) makes PostgREST reject the WHOLE upsert. Before this, that threw, _persistChanges
3207: // swallowed it as a quiet "schema mismatch" console.warn, and the row never reached
3208: // the cloud — so on reload the old row came back: sold reverted to listed, new items
3209: // vanished, and a return that hit a missing column was lost (double-counting the item).
3210: // These helpers strip the offending column(s) and retry so the row STILL saves (minus
3211: // the un-migrated field) and warn ONCE so the migration gets run. Generalises the
3212: // pre-existing account_* / trip-expenses one-off fallbacks.

3218:   if(mm) return mm[1];
3219:   return null;
3220: }

3711: // ============================================================
3712: // v2.21.50 — LOCAL WRITE-AHEAD OUTBOX (Phase 0 durability)
3713: // Mirrors every cloud write to localStorage BEFORE it is attempted; prunes it
3714: // once Supabase confirms. If a write never reaches the cloud (network drop, tab
3715: // evicted mid-save, reload before the in-tab auto-retry fires), the entry
3716: // survives on-device and replays on next boot. Supabase upserts/deletes are
3717: // idempotent, so replaying an already-saved write is a harmless no-op. This
3718: // only moves the same rows the diff engine already writes — it NEVER touches
3719: // the money/tax engine (calcGrossProfit / _saleBreakdown).
3720: // ============================================================
3721: const OUTBOX_KEY_BASE = 'retrade_outbox_v1';
3722: function _outboxKey(){ return OUTBOX_KEY_BASE + '_' + (_currentUserId || 'anon'); }
3723: function _outboxRead(){
3724:   try{ const raw = localStorage.getItem(_outboxKey()); return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {}; }
3725:   catch(e){ return {}; }
3726: }
3727: function _outboxSave(obj){
3728:   try{
3729:     if(!obj || Object.keys(obj).length === 0){ localStorage.removeItem(_outboxKey()); }
3730:     else { localStorage.setItem(_outboxKey(), JSON.stringify(obj)); }
3731:     return true;
3732:   }catch(e){ console.warn('[RETRADE] outbox write failed (quota?):', e && e.message); return false; }
3733: }
3734: function _outboxPendingCount(){ try{ return Object.keys(_outboxRead()).length; }catch(e){ return 0; } }

6081: function _avgPromoRate(){

6092: // v2.21.12 — unsold-item net estimate. LISTED items (real asking price already
6093: // set + real postage/shipping/packaging known) use the realistic estimate with
6094: // their actual charges (calcEstProfit). SOURCED items (only a guess) use a
6095: // conservative estimate: real fee model on the est price + estimated promo drag
6096: // + MEDIAN fulfilment drag. Always modest — never assumes zero fees/postage/promo.
6097: function _estPotentialGross(i){

6105:   const promo=est*_avgPromoRate()*1.20;
6106:   return +(est-bpf-promo-(i.costPrice||0)-calcPartsCost(i)-_medianFulfilmentDrag()).toFixed(2);
6107: }

6111:   return +(gross-_estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(i,gross)).toFixed(2);

6897: function highlightMatch(text,q){
6898:   if(!text||!q.trim())return esc(text||'');
6899:   const terms=q.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
6900:   let result=esc(text);
6901:   terms.forEach(t=>{
6902:     const escaped=t.replace(/[-\/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g,'\\$&');
6903:     const re=new RegExp('('+escaped+')','gi');
6904:     result=result.replace(re,'<mark>$1</mark>');
6905:   });
6906:   return result;
6907: }

9131:   const tripCost=runTrips.reduce(function(s,t){
9132:     return s+(t.mileage||0)*(t.ratePerMile||0.45)+((Array.isArray(t.expenses)?t.expenses:[]).reduce(function(a,e){return a+(e.amount||0);},0));
9133:   },0);

9138: function _runProfit(runId){
9139:   // Net profit = gross item profit minus all run overhead (trips + expenses)
9140:   const items=_runItems(runId);
9141:   const sold=items.filter(function(i){return i.state==='sold'||i.resaleSalePrice;});
9142:   const grossProfit=sold.reduce(function(s,i){return s+(calcGrossProfit(i)||0);},0);
9143:   const runTrips=(DB.trips||[]).filter(function(t){return t.sourcingRunId===runId;});
9144:   const runExps=(DB.expenses||[]).filter(function(e){return e.sourcingRunId===runId;});
9145:   const tripCost=runTrips.reduce(function(s,t){
9146:     return s+(t.mileage||0)*(t.ratePerMile||0.45)+((Array.isArray(t.expenses)?t.expenses:[]).reduce(function(a,e){return a+(e.amount||0);},0));
9147:   },0);
9148:   const expCost=runExps.reduce(function(s,e){return s+(e.amount||0);},0);
9149:   return grossProfit-tripCost-expCost;
9150: }

9158: // E33 — Accounts (partners/vendors). An account is a standing relationship
9159: // (unlike a Run, which is one time-boxed sourcing trip) — items tagged to it
9160: // can span any number of months and runs. Each item carries its own
9161: // accountSplitPercent (the partner's % share of THAT item's profit; null =
9162: // no split, item is just tracked under this account) and accountSettled
9163: // (whether their share has actually been paid out).
9164: // Canonical sold check for account reporting. Some older/imported items can have
9165: // a stale state='listed' even though their sale fields are populated. Accounts must
9166: // follow the financial evidence so sold items expose their real profit and settlement.
9167: function _accountItemIsSold(i){
9168:   // A full return means there is currently no completed sale to settle, even
9169:   // if historical resale fields remain for the audit trail.
9170:   return !!(i && !i.isReturned && (i.dateSold || i.resaleSalePrice || i.resaleDateSold || i.state==='sold'));
9171: }

9173: function _accountItems(accountId){

9178:     });
9179:   });
9180:   return items;
9181: }

9183: // Partner's owed amount on a single item: their % of that item's profit.
9184: // Returns null if the item hasn't sold yet (nothing owed on unsold stock) or
9185: // has no split % set (tracked but no money-split agreed).
9186: // What the partner is owed on a single sold item. Priority:
9187: //   1. accountPaidAmount — a manually-agreed figure (e.g. round £200 on £390
9188: //      profit), the source of truth when you'd rather not pay the exact split.
9189: //   2. accountSplitPercent — the calculated % of profit.
9190: // Returns null if the item isn't sold, or has neither a paid amount nor a %.
9191: // ── Account type resolution ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
9192: // Returns the effective type for an item: per-item override → account default
9193: // → 'supplier'. Types: 'consignment' | 'supplier' | 'hybrid'.
9194: //   consignment: partner gives you items, you split profit after sale, cost=0
9195: //   supplier:    you paid upfront (costPrice set), no ongoing split
9196: //   hybrid:      you paid something upfront AND agreed a profit split on top
9197: function _itemAccountType(i){
9198:   if(i.accountType)return i.accountType;
9199:   if(!i.accountId)return 'supplier';
9200:   const acct=_accounts.find(function(a){return a.id===i.accountId;});
9201:   return(acct&&acct.accountType)||'supplier';
9202: }
9203: 
9204: // Partner PROFIT split — used by calcNetProfit, tax engine, and SA103. Returns
9205: // the amount deducted FROM gross profit for the partner's cut. Supplier items
9206: // return null here because their cost is ALREADY inside calcGrossProfit — this
9207: // invariant keeps SA103 reconciliation penny-perfect. For settlement tracking
9208: // on the account page use _accountItemDebt() instead.
9209: function _accountItemOwed(i){
9210:   const isSold=_accountItemIsSold(i);
9211:   const effType=_itemAccountType(i);
9212:   // A paid consignment/hybrid settlement is a real sunk economic cost even if
9213:   // the buyer later returns the unit. Keep that paid amount in lifetime P&L
9214:   // while the item is returned, and allocate the SAME total across Sale 1/2 if
9215:   // it is resold. This prevents both the returned-state disappearance and a
9216:   // second partner charge on resale. Supplier acquisition cost is deliberately
9217:   // handled separately as COGS and must never enter this path.
9218:   if(!isSold){
9219:     if(effType!=='supplier'&&i.accountSettled===true){
9220:       if(i.accountPaidAmount!=null)return +Math.max(0,Number(i.accountPaidAmount)||0).toFixed(2);
9221:       try{
9222:         const st=_findPaidSettlementForItem(i.id);
9223:         if(st&&st.tx){
9224:           const alloc=(st.tx.items||[]).find(function(a){return a&&a.id===i.id;});
9225:           if(alloc&&alloc.amount!=null)return +Math.max(0,Number(alloc.amount)||0).toFixed(2);
9226:         }
9227:       }catch(e){}
9228:     }
9229:     return null;
9230:   }
9231:   // v2.19.28 — LOCKED INVARIANT, now enforced HERE rather than only at the
9232:   // write sites. A supplier's cost is ALREADY inside calcGrossProfit via
9233:   // costPrice. Returning non-null for one deducts it a SECOND time in
9234:   // calcNetProfit and misclassifies it into SA103 Box 17 (partner) when it
9235:   // belongs in COGS — two boxes wrong, silently. The item split panel can set
9236:   // accountType='supplier' alongside a % or a fixed amount (and will not save
9237:   // without one), so a write-site guard cannot hold the line. Settlement
9238:   // tracking is _accountItemDebt()'s job and is deliberately separate.
9239:   if(effType==='supplier')return null;
9240:   if(i.accountPaidAmount!=null)return +Number(i.accountPaidAmount).toFixed(2);
9241:   if(i.accountSplitPercent==null)return null;
9242:   const profit=calcGrossProfit(i);
9243:   if(profit===null)return null;
9244:   return+(profit*(i.accountSplitPercent/100)).toFixed(2);
9245: }
9246: // Total unpaid partner DEBT for settlement tracking on the account page.
9247: // Includes supplier cost (which is what you owe for the stock) and returns
9248: // non-null even on unsold supplier items — you owe a supplier at purchase, not
9249: // sale. For consignment/hybrid this is the same as _accountItemOwed().
9250: function _accountItemDebt(i){
9251:   const effType=_itemAccountType(i);
9252:   if(effType==='supplier'){
9253:     if(i.accountPaidAmount!=null)return +Number(i.accountPaidAmount).toFixed(2);
9254:     const cost=+(i.costPrice||0);
9255:     if(cost>0)return +cost.toFixed(2);
9256:     return null;
9257:   }
9258:   return _accountItemOwed(i);
9259: }

9643: function _roundMoney(v){return Math.round((Number(v)||0)*100)/100;}
9644: function _allocatePartnerTotal(items,total){
9645:   const weights=items.map(function(i){return Math.max(0,calcGrossProfit(i)||0);});
9646:   const sum=weights.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0);
9647:   let remaining=_roundMoney(total);
9648:   return items.map(function(i,idx){
9649:     let amount;
9650:     if(idx===items.length-1) amount=remaining;
9651:     else {
9652:       amount=sum>0?_roundMoney(total*(weights[idx]/sum)):_roundMoney(total/items.length);
9653:       remaining=_roundMoney(remaining-amount);
9654:     }
9655:     return {id:i.id,month:i._month,amount:Math.max(0,amount),kind:_itemAccountType(i)};
9656:   });
9657: }

12509: function showCategoryItems(cat,_fromBack){
12510:   let items=[];

12512:     if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return;

12516:   const active=items.filter(i=>!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt);
12517:   items.sort((a,b)=>{
12518:     const statusOrder=i=>{
12519:       if(!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt)return 0;
12520:       if((i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)&&!i.isReturned)return 1;
12521:       return 2;
12522:     };
12523:     const so=statusOrder(a)-statusOrder(b);
12524:     if(so!==0)return so;
12525:     if(statusOrder(a)===0){
12526:       const _today=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
12527:       const dA=a.dateListed?daysBetween(a.dateListed,_today):0;
12528:       const dB=b.dateListed?daysBetween(b.dateListed,_today):0;
12529:       return dB-dA;
12530:     }
12531:     const dateA=(a.resaleDateSold||a.dateSold||'');
12532:     const dateB=(b.resaleDateSold||b.dateSold||'');
12533:     return dateB.localeCompare(dateA);
12534:   });
12535:   const totalProfit=sold.reduce((s,i)=>s+calcNetProfit(i),0);

12546:         <span style="color:var(--muted)">${active.length} active</span>
12547:         ${avgDays!==null?`<span style="color:var(--text-secondary)">avg ${avgDays}d to sell</span>`:''}
12548:       </div>
12549:     </div>

12551:       const _today=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
12552:       const status=i.scrappedAt?(i.scrapReason==='donated'?'donated':'scrapped'):i.isReturned?'returned':i.resaleSalePrice?'resold':i.dateSold?'sold':i.condition==='spares'?'spares':'listed';

12592: function _inSummaryPeriod(dateStr){
12593:   const range=_periodDateRange(SUMMARY_PERIOD);
12594:   if(!range.from)return true;
12595:   if(!dateStr)return false;
12596:   return dateStr>=range.from&&dateStr<=range.to;
12597: }

12599: function showROIItems(){
12600:   let items=[];
12601:   allDBKeys().forEach(k=>{(DB[k]||[]).forEach(i=>{
12602:     if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return;
12603:     if((i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)&&!i.isReturned){
12604:       const saleDate=i.resaleSalePrice?i.resaleDateSold:i.dateSold;
12605:       if(!_inSummaryPeriod(saleDate))return;
12606:       const roi=calcROI(i);if(roi!==null)items.push({...i,month:k,_roi:roi});
12607:     }

12712: function _snapshotSoldItems(){
12713:   // Dashboard drill-downs must use the exact same Sale-N transaction source as
12714:   // calcYearlyStats(). A physical item can therefore appear more than once in
12715:   // the selected period (Sale 1, Sale 2, Sale 3...) without earlier cycles
12716:   // disappearing when the item is resold again.
12717:   const range=_periodDateRange(SUMMARY_PERIOD);

12719:     .filter(function(ev){return !ev.isReturnAdjustment;})
12720:     .map(function(ev){

12722:       return Object.assign({},ev.item,{
12723:         month:ev.month,

12843:     // Canonical dated transaction feed. This is deliberately the SAME source as
12844:     // the headline: Sale 1/2/3+ and return adjustments are separate events.

12846:       const isReturn=!!ev.isReturnAdjustment;
12847:       const saleNo=isReturn?Math.max(1,Number(ev.returnEntry&&ev.returnEntry.saleNo)||1):Math.max(1,Number(ev.sale)||1);
12848:       const b=_saleBreakdown(ev);

12852:         _event:ev,_entryType:isReturn?'return':'sale',_saleNo:saleNo,
12853:         _displayDate:ev.saleDate||'',_displayPrice:+revenue.toFixed(2),
12854:         _entryProfit:Number(b.netProfit)||0,_margin:isReturn?null:ev.margin,
12855:         _platform:b.platform||''
12856:       });
12857:     });
12858:   }else{
12859:     allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){(DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
12860:       if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return;
12861:       const onHand=!i.scrappedAt&&(i.isReturned||(!i.dateSold&&!i.resaleSalePrice));
12862:       if(filter==='unrealised'&&onHand&&!i.isReturned)items.push(Object.assign({},i,{month:k}));

12872:   const saleCount=items.filter(function(i){return i._entryType==='sale';}).length;
12873:   const returnCount=items.filter(function(i){return i._entryType==='return';}).length;
12874:

12957:   if(filter==='all'){STOCK_STATE_FILTER='all';STOCK_FILTER='all';STOCK_SOURCED_FILTER='all';}

15813: function _jobLotEstimatedNetProfit(lot){
15814:   const gross=_jobLotEstimatedProfit(lot);
15815:   if(gross===null||gross===undefined)return null;
15816:   const members=_jobLotMembers(lot.id,false);
15817:   if(!members.length)return +Number(gross).toFixed(2);
15818:   let net=0;
15819:   members.forEach(function(x){
15820:     const share=+(Number(gross)*_jobLotMemberWeightShare(x.item,lot)).toFixed(2);
15821:     net+=share-_estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(x.item,share);
15822:   });
15823:   return +net.toFixed(2);
15824: }
15825: function _jobLotAllocation(total,buyerPost,shipping,packaging,weights){

15828:     sale:_bundleSplit(Number(total)||0,safe),
15829:     postage:_bundleSplit(Number(buyerPost)||0,safe),
15830:     shipping:_bundleSplit(Number(shipping)||0,safe),
15831:     packaging:_bundleSplit(Number(packaging)||0,safe)
15832:   };

15834: function _applyJobLotSaleLeg(i,leg){
15835:   const hasFull=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1;});
15836:   if(hasFull){
15837:     // A returned physical unit is a Sale-2 leg. Never overwrite Sale 1 — and
15838:     // never reuse Sale 1's bundle fields for this new order. Otherwise the
15839:     // historical Sale 1 event would appear inside today's Job Lot.
15840:     i.resaleDateSold=leg.date;i.resaleSalePrice=leg.salePrice;i.resalePostage=leg.postage||0;
15841:     i.resaleShippingCost=leg.shipping||0;i.resalePackagingCost=leg.packaging||0;
15842:     i.resalePromoPercent=leg.promo||0;i.resalePlatform=leg.platform;i.isReturned=false;i.state='sold';
15843:     i.resaleBundleId=leg.bundleId;i.resaleBundleRef=leg.bundleRef;
15844:     i.resaleBundleTotal=+Number(leg.bundleTotal||0).toFixed(2);
15845:   }else{
15846:     i.dateSold=leg.date;i.salePrice=leg.salePrice;i.postage=leg.postage||0;
15847:     i.shippingCost=leg.shipping||0;i.packagingCost=leg.packaging||0;
15848:     i.promoPercent=leg.promo||0;i.soldOnPlatform=leg.platform;i.salePlatform=leg.platform;i.isReturned=false;i.state='sold';
15849:     i.bundleId=leg.bundleId;i.bundleRef=leg.bundleRef;i.bundleTotal=+Number(leg.bundleTotal||0).toFixed(2);
15850:   }
15851:   try{i.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(i);}catch(e){}
15852:   return i;
15853: }
15854: function _canAddToJobLot(i){
15855:   if(!i||i.scrappedAt||_activeJobLotMembership(i.id))return false;
15856:   if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return false;
15857:   // Physical unit must be in hand: unsold first-sale stock OR a fully returned
15858:   // unit awaiting a second decision. A completed resale is not in inventory.
15859:   if(i.isReturned)return true;
15860:   if(i.resaleSalePrice)return false;
15861:   return !i.dateSold;
15862: }

16743: function _selectionTotals(entries){
16744:   let count=0,soldCount=0,unsoldCount=0,returnedCount=0;
16745:   let cost=0,revenue=0,realisedProfit=0,potentialProfit=0,estValue=0;
16746:   let soldRev=0,soldProfit=0,daysSum=0,daysCount=0;
16747:   (entries||[]).forEach(function(e){
16748:     if(!e)return;
16749:     count++;
16750:     if(_selEntryIsEvent(e)){
16751:       // Month sale-event: use the event's OWN leg figures so a selection/filter
16752:       // total reconciles exactly to calcMonthStatsBySale (event .profit already
16753:       // routes through _reconciledItemEventProfits).
16754:       const _br=_saleBreakdown(e);
16755:       const rev=e.isReturnAdjustment?(Number(e.salePrice)||0):((Number(_br.salePrice)||0)+(Number(_br.postage)||0));
16756:       revenue+=rev; realisedProfit+=(Number(_br.netProfit)||0);
16757:       if(e.isReturnAdjustment||e.isReturned){
16758:         returnedCount++;
16759:       } else {
16760:         soldCount++; soldRev+=rev; soldProfit+=(Number(_br.netProfit)||0);
16761:         if(e.daysToSell!=null){daysSum+=e.daysToSell;daysCount++;}
16762:       }
16763:     } else {
16764:       // Raw item.
16765:       const it=e;
16766:       const c=(it.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(it); cost+=c;
16767:       const isSold=!!(it.dateSold||it.resaleSalePrice)&&!it.isReturned;
16768:       if(isSold){
16769:         soldCount++;
16770:         const rev=calcItemRevenue(it), gp=calcGrossProfit(it);
16771:         revenue+=rev; realisedProfit+=(gp||0); soldRev+=rev; soldProfit+=(gp||0);
16772:         const d=calcDaysToSell(it); if(d!=null){daysSum+=d;daysCount++;}
16773:       } else if(it.isReturned){
16774:         returnedCount++;
16775:       } else {
16776:         unsoldCount++;
16777:         const pn=_estPotentialNet(it); if(pn!=null)potentialProfit+=pn;
16778:         estValue+=(it.salePrice||it.estSalePrice||0);
16779:       }
16780:     }
16781:   });
16782:   // Blended margin over the SOLD legs only (Σprofit / Σrevenue — never an avg of
16783:   // per-item margins). Headline realisedProfit nets returns; margin does not.
16784:   const margin = soldRev>0 ? +(soldProfit/soldRev*100).toFixed(1) : null;
16785:   const avgDays = daysCount>0 ? Math.round(daysSum/daysCount) : null;
16786:   return {count:count,soldCount:soldCount,unsoldCount:unsoldCount,returnedCount:returnedCount,
16787:           cost:+cost.toFixed(2),revenue:+revenue.toFixed(2),realisedProfit:+realisedProfit.toFixed(2),
16788:           potentialProfit:+potentialProfit.toFixed(2),estValue:+estValue.toFixed(2),
16789:           margin:margin,avgDays:avgDays};
16790: }

18069: // ── SCRAP / WRITE-OFF ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
18070: // A returned item Oskar won't relist gets a terminal 'scrapped' flag. Its buy
18071: // cost was already booked as COGS when the original sale it came back from was
18072: // recorded (SA103 Box 17, at the sale month), so scrapping re-books NOTHING —
18073: // it only writes scrappedAt/scrapReason and strips any phantom resale postage.
18074: // Tax-neutral by construction (proven: phase0_scrap_test.js). Reason 'joblot'
18075: // pre-stages the job-lot merge (built separately).
18076: function _canScrap(i){
18077:   if(!i||i.scrappedAt)return false;
18078:   if(_activeJobLotMembership(i.id))return false;
18079:   if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return false;
18080:   // Removable unless it's currently sold/resold AND not back in your hands. isReturned always
18081:   // wins (a returned item is on hand regardless of prior sale/resale or an odd leftover state).
18082:   return !((i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)&&!i.isReturned);
18083: }

19471: function saveRelistAuditEdits(){

19476:     const m=parts[0],id=parts[1];

19479:       const f=input.getAttribute('data-orphan-field');
19480:       const v=parseFloat(input.value);
19481:       if(isNaN(v))return;
19482:       if(f==='promoPercent')values[f]=v/100;
19483:       else values[f]=v;
19484:     });

19486:   });

19493: // Scans all items for errors and warnings. Returns {errors:[], warnings:[], clean:0}.
19494: // Each finding: {severity:'error'|'warning', code:string, label:string, desc:string, m:string, id:string, name:string}
19495: function _isValidStoredDate(v){
19496:   if(!v)return false;
19497:   const d=String(v).slice(0,10);
19498:   if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d))return false;
19499:   const parts=d.split('-').map(Number), y=parts[0],m=parts[1],day=parts[2];
19500:   const dt=new Date(Date.UTC(y,m-1,day));
19501:   return dt.getUTCFullYear()===y&&dt.getUTCMonth()===(m-1)&&dt.getUTCDate()===day;
19502: }

21060: // v1.4.6 — Canonical lifecycle state machine. All primary item actions use
21061: // this single resolver so List / Mark Sold / Return / Relist do not drift apart.
21062: function _itemLifecycleState(i){
21063:   if(!i)return 'missing';
21064:   if(i.scrappedAt)return 'disposed';
21065:   if(i.isReturned)return 'returned';
21066:   if(i.resaleSalePrice||i.dateSold)return 'sold';
21067:   if(i.state==='sourced')return 'sourced';
21068:   return 'listed';
21069: }
21070: function _lifecycleCan(i,action){
21071:   const s=_itemLifecycleState(i);
21072:   if(action==='list')return s==='sourced';
21073:   if(action==='markSold')return s==='listed';
21074:   if(action==='return')return s==='sold';
21075:   if(action==='relist')return s==='returned';
21076:   if(action==='moveUnlisted')return s==='returned';
21077:   return false;
21078: }

21508: function duplicateItem(m,id,context){
21509:   const item=(DB[m]||[]).find(i=>i.id===id);
21510:   if(!item)return;
21511:   const copy=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(item));
21512:   copy.id=_newId('dup');
21513:   copy.gid='R-'+String(getNextGID()).padStart(4,'0');
21514:   copy.dateListed=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
21515:   // v2.09.2 — Force a clean "active listing" state. Previously only dateSold /
21516:   // grossProfit / isReturned were cleared, so duplicating a sold-then-relisted
21517:   // item left resaleSalePrice / resaleDateSold intact — the Account page's
21518:   // sold-filter (i.state==='sold' || !!i.resaleSalePrice) then treated the copy
21519:   // as a sale even though the Stock Room showed it correctly as listed.
21520:   copy.state='listed';
21521:   copy.dateSold=null;copy.grossProfit=null;copy.isReturned=false;
21522:   copy.resaleSalePrice=null;copy.resaleDateSold=null;copy.resaleGrossProfit=null;
21523:   // v2.19.40 — a clone starts as a fresh Sale 1, so it must not inherit the
21524:   // original's per-sale platforms. soldOnPlatform (sale-time platform) and
21525:   // resalePlatform (sale-2 platform) were left set, so a clone of an item sold
21526:   // on a non-default platform was mispriced off that stale platform's fees.
21527:   copy.soldOnPlatform=null;copy.resalePlatform=null;
21528:   // v2.19.26 — the clone inherits resaleListingFee, which calcGrossProfit now
21529:   // charges unconditionally. Left uncleared the duplicate would be billed for
21530:   // the ORIGINAL's relist. Clear it, then stamp a fresh sale-1 fee: a
21531:   // duplicate is a new listing and eBay bills it as one.
21532:   copy.resaleListingFee=0;
21533:   _stampListingFee(copy);
21534:   copy.accountSettled=false; // new item = new debt to the partner
21535:   delete copy.bundleId;delete copy.bundleRef;delete copy.bundleTotal;
21536:   delete copy.resaleBundleId;delete copy.resaleBundleRef;delete copy.resaleBundleTotal;
21537:   copy.returnHistory=[];delete copy.returnData;
21538:   delete copy.scrappedAt;delete copy.scrapReason;delete copy.scrapNote; // v2.21.36 — a clone is a fresh live listing, never born scrapped
21539:   const stripSuffix=function(name){
21540:     const m2=(name||'').match(/^(.+?) - (\d+)$/);
21541:     return m2?m2[1]:(name||'');
21542:   };
21543:   const rootId=item.parentId||item.id;
21544:   const rootItem=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===rootId);

21704: function confirmRelist(m,id){
21705:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
21706:   if(!i)return;
21707:   const price=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-price').value)||i.salePrice||0;
21708:   const promoInput=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-promo').value)||0;
21709:   // E3 P1 #3 — Capture sale 2 packaging from the relist modal so it isn't
21710:   // silently inherited from sale 1. The input pre-fills with i.packagingCost
21711:   // (the sale 1 value) so the user can adjust if they used a different
21712:   // mailer for the relist. Empty / NaN falls back to sale 1's value.
21713:   const _pkgRaw=document.getElementById('relist-packaging');
21714:   const newPackaging=_pkgRaw&&_pkgRaw.value!==''?(parseFloat(_pkgRaw.value)||0):(i.packagingCost||0);
21715:   const condition=window._relistCondition||'working';
21716:   const postMode=window._relistPostMode||'simple';
21717:   // Per-resale platform — the relist can be on a different platform than sale 1
21718:   // (e.g. sold on eBay private, returned, relisted on eBay Business). The resale
21719:   // fee in calcGrossProfit reads i.resalePlatform so sale-2 fees are correct.
21720:   const _relistPlatEl=document.getElementById('relist-platform');
21721:   const _newRelistPlatform=(_relistPlatEl&&_relistPlatEl.value&&PLATFORMS[_relistPlatEl.value])?_relistPlatEl.value:(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i));
21722:   if(_newRelistPlatform&&PLATFORMS[_newRelistPlatform]){
21723:     i.resalePlatform=_newRelistPlatform;
21724:     // The relisted item is now an ACTIVE listing on the chosen platform, so its
21725:     // live P&L must use that platform's fees — not sale 1's. Update the item's
21726:     // own platform and clear the prior soldOnPlatform so _itemPlatform resolves
21727:     // to the new one. resalePlatform is also kept for the resale fee path.
21728:     i.defaultPlatform=_relistPlatEl.value;
21729:     i.soldOnPlatform=null;
21730:   }
21731:   // E3 P0 #1 — Snapshot sale 1 economics into the latest returnHistory entry
21732:   // BEFORE we overwrite shipping/postage/sdRate/promo with relist values.
21733:   // Without this, the original sale's costs are destroyed and calcGrossProfit
21734:   // ends up double-counting relist shipping (once as i.shippingCost, once as
21735:   // resaleShippingCost) when the resale completes. _shippingCostAtRelist is
21736:   // the marker name — calcGrossProfit reads these on the resold-after-return
21737:   // path. Items that were relisted before this fix don't have the snapshot;
21738:   // they fall back to the live (now-overwritten) values, so older relisted
21739:   // items are still slightly wrong but new relists from this point are correct.
21740:   const _relistReturnEntry=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&!r._relistedAt;})||null;
21741:   if(_relistReturnEntry){
21742:     if(_relistReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist=i.shippingCost||0;
21743:     if(_relistReturnEntry._postageAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._postageAtRelist=i.postage||0;
21744:     if(_relistReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist=i.packagingCost||0;
21745:     if(_relistReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist=i.promoPercent||0;
21746:     if(_relistReturnEntry._sdRateIdxAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._sdRateIdxAtRelist=(i._sdRateIdx!=null?i._sdRateIdx:1);
21747:     if(_relistReturnEntry._sdBuyerPaysAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._sdBuyerPaysAtRelist=!!i._sdBuyerPays;
21748:     if(_relistReturnEntry._salePriceAtRelist===undefined)_relistReturnEntry._salePriceAtRelist=i.salePrice||0;
21749:   }
21750:   // If the return belongs to Sale 2+, that completed failed resale is now
21751:   // fully frozen on its returnHistory row. Clear the single live resale slot so
21752:   // the relisted unit is genuinely active again and can become Sale 3/4/etc.
21753:   // Sale 1 fields are never cleared because they remain the item's original
21754:   // acquisition/sale anchor.
21755:   if(_relistReturnEntry && (Number(_relistReturnEntry.saleNo)||1)>=2){
21756:     i.resaleSalePrice=null;
21757:     i.resaleDateSold=null;
21758:     i.resalePlatform=null;
21759:     i.resalePostage=null;
21760:     i.resaleShippingCost=null;
21761:     i.resalePackagingCost=null;
21762:     i.resalePromoPercent=null;
21763:     i.resaleGrossProfit=null;
21764:     i.resaleBundleId=null;i.resaleBundleRef=null;i.resaleBundleTotal=null;
21765:     // The completed Sale-N slot was cleared above; restore the NEW listing's
21766:     // platform so Sale N+1 does not silently fall back to the previous/default one.
21767:     i.resalePlatform=_newRelistPlatform;
21768:   }
21769:   // Apply postage settings
21770:   i._postageMode=postMode;
21771:   if(postMode==='simple'){
21772:     let sdIdx=parseInt(document.getElementById('relist-sd-rate').value);if(isNaN(sdIdx))sdIdx=1;
21773:     const buyerPays=!document.getElementById('relist-sd-free-postage').checked;
21774:     const rateCost=_sdRateFor(sdIdx,price);
21775:     i._sdRateIdx=sdIdx;
21776:     i._sdBuyerPays=buyerPays;
21777:     i.shippingCost=rateCost;
21778:     i.postage=buyerPays?rateCost:0;
21779:   } else {
21780:     i.shippingCost=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-shipping-cost').value)||0;
21781:     i.postage=parseFloat(document.getElementById('relist-postage').value)||0;
21782:   }
21783:   // Restore to active stock
21784:   i.isReturned=false;
21785:   i.state='listed';
21786:   // v2.19.26 — a relist is a second billable listing. Stamped here, after the
21787:   // resalePlatform/defaultPlatform assignment above, so a return-then-relist
21788:   // onto a DIFFERENT platform is priced on the platform it actually went to.
21789:   const _thisRelistFee=_listingFeeFor(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i));
21790:   _stampRelistFee(i);
21791:   i.dateSold=null;
21792:   i.salePrice=price;
21793:   i.promoPercent=promoInput/100;
21794:   i.packagingCost=newPackaging;
21795:   i.condition=condition==='working'?undefined:'spares';
21796:   i.grossProfit=null;
21797:   // Log relist in returnHistory for audit trail
21798:   if(_relistReturnEntry){

21800:     _relistReturnEntry._relistedAt=relistDate;
21801:     _relistReturnEntry._relistCondition=condition;
21802:     _relistReturnEntry._platformAtRelist=i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
21803:     _relistReturnEntry._listingFeeAtRelist=_thisRelistFee;
21804:     // v2.15.0 — register the relist in the maintenance log. Two reasons:
21805:     //  1. The item-page relist COUNTER only reads refreshHistory, so a
21806:     //     relist-after-return never registered. That's why it was being logged
21807:     //     manually as well — which produced a second "Relisted" timeline row
21808:     //     for one real event (the reported double-log).
21809:     //  2. _effectiveListDate resets the freshness clock from _lastRelistAt.
21810:     //     Without this, a relisted item kept its ORIGINAL dateListed age and
21811:     //     drifted into the wrong Stock Room aging bucket.
21812:     // _fromReturn marks this entry as owned by the returnHistory _relistedAt
21813:     // record: the timeline skips it and renders the richer _relistedAt row
21814:     // (which carries condition + price), so one relist stays one row.
21815:     if(!i.refreshHistory)i.refreshHistory=[];
21816:     i.refreshHistory.push({type:'relist',date:relistDate,_fromReturn:true});
21817:     i._lastRelistAt=relistDate;
21818:   }
21819:   saveDB();
21820:   window._relistCondition=null;
21821:   window._relistPostMode=null;
21822:   closePanel();
21823:   toast('Relisted as '+(condition==='spares'?'Spares / Repairs':'Working'));
21824:   const cur=document.querySelector('.page.on').id;
21825:   if(cur==='p-item')renderItemPage(m,id);
21826:   else if(cur==='p-monthly')renderMonth();
21827:   else if(cur==='p-stock')renderStock();
21828:   else renderSummary();
21829: }

21831: function _currentResaleSaleNo(i){
21832:   // Sale 1 uses the base fields. Every later completed sale reuses the live
21833:   // `resale*` slot, while its full-return record freezes that cycle in
21834:   // returnHistory. The most recent relisted full return therefore tells us
21835:   // which numbered resale is currently live (Sale 2, 3, 4, ...).
21836:   const relistedFull=(i&&i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){
21837:     return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt;
21838:   });
21839:   let max=1;
21840:   relistedFull.forEach(function(r){max=Math.max(max,Number(r.saleNo)||1);});
21841:   return Math.max(2,max+1);
21842: }
21843: function _currentSaleForReturn(i){
21844:   const hasResale=!!(i&&i.resaleSalePrice);
21845:   const saleNo=hasResale?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):1;
21846:   const isResale=saleNo>=2;
21847:   const price=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?i.resaleSalePrice:i.salePrice)||0);
21848:   const date=(isResale?i.resaleDateSold:i.dateSold)||null;
21849:   const postage=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resalePostage!=null?i.resalePostage:i.postage):i.postage)||0);
21850:   const platformId=isResale?(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i)):_s1Platform(i);
21851:   const promoPercent=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?i.resalePromoPercent:i.promoPercent)||0);
21852:   const shippingCost=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resaleShippingCost!=null?i.resaleShippingCost:i.shippingCost):i.shippingCost)||0);
21853:   const packagingCost=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?(i.resalePackagingCost!=null?i.resalePackagingCost:i.packagingCost):i.packagingCost)||0);
21854:   let listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(isResale?0:i.listingFee)||0);
21855:   if(isResale){
21856:     const prior=((i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===saleNo-1&&r._relistedAt;}));
21857:     if(prior&&prior._listingFeeAtRelist!=null)listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(prior._listingFeeAtRelist)||0);
21858:     else listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(i.resaleListingFee)||0); // live Sale 2/3/4+ fallback
21859:   }
21860:   const bundleId=isResale?(i.resaleBundleId||null):(i.bundleId||null);
21861:   const bundleRef=isResale?(i.resaleBundleRef||''):(i.bundleRef||'');
21862:   const bundleTotal=Number(isResale?i.resaleBundleTotal:i.bundleTotal)||0;
21863:   return {saleNo:saleNo,is2:isResale,price:price,date:date,postage:postage,platformId:platformId,promoPercent:promoPercent,shippingCost:shippingCost,packagingCost:packagingCost,listingFee:listingFee,bundleId:bundleId,bundleRef:bundleRef,bundleTotal:bundleTotal};
21864: }

21875:   const html=`

21938:     _postageAtReturn:cur.postage,
21939:     _platformAtReturn:cur.platformId,
21940:     _promoPercentAtReturn:cur.promoPercent,
21941:     _shippingCostAtReturn:cur.shippingCost,
21942:     _packagingCostAtReturn:cur.packagingCost,
21943:     _listingFeeAtReturn:cur.listingFee,
21944:     _bundleIdAtReturn:cur.bundleId||undefined,
21945:     _bundleRefAtReturn:cur.bundleRef||undefined,
21946:     _bundleTotalAtReturn:cur.bundleTotal||undefined
21947:   };
21948:   // For Sale 2+ freeze the live resale fulfilment now. These fields already
21949:   // round-trip through item_returns and let a later relist clear the live
21950:   // resale slot without losing the failed cycle's economics.
21951:   if(_saleNo>=2){
21952:     entry._salePriceAtRelist=cur.price;
21953:     entry._postageAtRelist=cur.postage;
21954:     entry._shippingCostAtRelist=Number(i.resaleShippingCost||0);
21955:     entry._packagingCostAtRelist=Number(i.resalePackagingCost||0);
21956:     entry._promoPercentAtRelist=Number(i.resalePromoPercent||0);
21957:   }
21958: 
21959:   if(!i.returnHistory)i.returnHistory=[];
21960: 
21961:   // A full refund supersedes adjustments for THIS sale only. Preserve the
21962:   // previous sale cycle's history (e.g. Sale 1 partials/full return when Sale 2
21963:   // is later returned). Also replace any duplicate full return on this sale.
21964:   if(type==='full_seller'||type==='full_ebay'){
21965:     i.returnHistory=i.returnHistory.filter(function(r){
21966:       const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
21967:       return rs!==_saleNo;
21968:     });
21969:   }
21970: 
21971:   i.returnHistory.push(entry);
21972: 
21973:   // Mark as fully returned only for full refund types
21974:   if(type==='full_seller'||type==='full_ebay'){
21975:     i.isReturned=true;
21976:     i.state='returned';
21977:     i.dateSold=null; // remove from sold — back to stock
21978:   }
21979:   saveDB();
21980:   toast('Return logged');
21981:   const _activePage=document.querySelector('.page.on');
21982:   const _activeId=_activePage?_activePage.id:'p-summary';
21983:   closePanel();
21984:   if(_activeId==='p-item'){
21985:     // Already on the item page — re-render in place after panel slides out.
21986:     // Do NOT call openItemPage('p-item') or exitItemPage()'s back button breaks.
21987:     setTimeout(function(){ renderItemPage(m,id); }, 340);
21988:   } else {
21989:     // Coming from a list page — navigate to item page after panel clears.
21990:     setTimeout(function(){ openItemPage(m,id,_activeId); }, 340);
21991:   }
21992: }
21993: 
21994: function _removeReturnEntry(m,id,rtnIdx){
21995:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
21996:   if(!i||!Array.isArray(i.returnHistory))return null;
21997:   const r=i.returnHistory[rtnIdx];
21998:   if(!r)return null;
21999:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
22000:   const isFull=(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
22001: 
22002:   // Every full return is the structural bridge to the NEXT listing cycle.
22003:   // Never let a user delete Sale N's return while its relist or a later sale
22004:   // still exists — that would create Sale N+1 with no originating return.
22005:   const _laterFrozen=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r2){
22006:     return r2!==r&&(r2.type==='full_seller'||r2.type==='full_ebay')&&Math.max(1,Number(r2.saleNo)||1)>saleNo;
22007:   });
22008:   const _liveNo=i.resaleSalePrice?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):null;
22009:   const _laterLive=!!(_liveNo&&_liveNo>saleNo);
22010:   if(isFull&&(r._relistedAt||_laterFrozen||_laterLive)){
22011:     const _later=_laterFrozen||_laterLive;
22012:     toast(_later
22013:       ?'Undo the later sale/relist first, then remove Sale '+saleNo+' return.'
22014:       :'Undo the relist first, then remove Sale '+saleNo+' return.','error');
22015:     return null;
22016:   }
22017: 
22018:   i.returnHistory.splice(rtnIdx,1);
22019:   const stillFullyReturned=i.returnHistory.some(function(r2){
22020:     return (r2.type==='full_seller'||r2.type==='full_ebay')&&!r2._relistedAt;
22021:   });
22022:   if(!stillFullyReturned&&i.isReturned){
22023:     i.isReturned=false;
22024:     i.state='sold';
22025:     if(saleNo===1){
22026:       // Sale 1 full return cleared dateSold; restore its original date.
22027:       if(!i.dateSold)i.dateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||null;
22028:     }else{
22029:       // Sale 2 remains represented by resaleSalePrice/resaleDateSold. Never
22030:       // copy the resale date into dateSold — that field belongs to Sale 1.
22031:       i.dateSold=null;
22032:     }
22033:   }
22034:   saveDB();
22035:   return r;
22036: }

22038: // v1.4.6 — Dependency-aware return undo. If the only later structural step
22039: // is this return's relist, undo both atomically and restore Sale N as sold.
22040: function _dropReturnOwnedRelistMaintenance(i,r){
22041:   if(!i||!r||!r._relistedAt||!Array.isArray(i.refreshHistory))return;
22042:   const d=String(r._relistedAt).slice(0,10);
22043:   for(let k=i.refreshHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
22044:     const e=i.refreshHistory[k];
22045:     if(e&&e.type==='relist'&&e._fromReturn&&String(e.date||'').slice(0,10)===d){i.refreshHistory.splice(k,1);break;}
22046:   }
22047:   const prior=i.refreshHistory.slice().reverse().find(function(e){return e&&e.type==='relist';});
22048:   i._lastRelistAt=prior?prior.date:null;
22049: }
22050: function _restoreCompletedSaleFromReturn(i,r){
22051:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
22052:   const num=function(v,f){return v!=null?(Number(v)||0):(Number(f)||0);};
22053:   if(saleNo===1){
22054:     i.salePrice=num(r._salePriceAtReturn,i.salePrice);i.dateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||i.dateSold||null;
22055:     i.postage=num(r._postageAtReturn,i.postage);i.shippingCost=num(r._shippingCostAtReturn,i.shippingCost);
22056:     i.packagingCost=num(r._packagingCostAtReturn,i.packagingCost);i.promoPercent=num(r._promoPercentAtReturn,i.promoPercent);
22057:     if(r._platformAtReturn)i.soldOnPlatform=r._platformAtReturn;
22058:     i.resaleSalePrice=null;i.resaleDateSold=null;i.resaleGrossProfit=null;i.resalePostage=null;i.resaleShippingCost=null;i.resalePackagingCost=null;i.resalePromoPercent=null;
22059:   }else{
22060:     i.resaleSalePrice=num(r._salePriceAtReturn,r._salePriceAtRelist);i.resaleDateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||null;
22061:     i.resalePostage=num(r._postageAtReturn,r._postageAtRelist);i.resaleShippingCost=num(r._shippingCostAtReturn,r._shippingCostAtRelist);
22062:     i.resalePackagingCost=num(r._packagingCostAtReturn,r._packagingCostAtRelist);i.resalePromoPercent=num(r._promoPercentAtReturn,r._promoPercentAtRelist);
22063:     i.resaleListingFee=num(r._listingFeeAtReturn,i.resaleListingFee);i.resalePlatform=r._platformAtReturn||i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
22064:     i.salePrice=i.resaleSalePrice;i.postage=i.resalePostage;i.shippingCost=i.resaleShippingCost;i.packagingCost=i.resalePackagingCost;i.promoPercent=i.resalePromoPercent;
22065:   }
22066:   i.isReturned=false;i.state='sold';i.condition=undefined;i.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(i);
22067: }
22068: async function undoReturnCascade(m,id,rtnIdx){
22069:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
22070:   const r=i&&Array.isArray(i.returnHistory)?i.returnHistory[rtnIdx]:null;if(!r)return;
22071:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);const isFull=r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';
22072:   if(!isFull||!r._relistedAt)return undoReturnEntry(m,id,rtnIdx);
22073:   const laterReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(x){return x!==r&&(x.type==='full_seller'||x.type==='full_ebay')&&Math.max(1,Number(x.saleNo)||1)>saleNo;});
22074:   const liveSaleNo=i.resaleSalePrice?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):0;
22075:   if(laterReturn||(liveSaleNo&&liveSaleNo>saleNo)){toast('A later sale exists. Undo the newest sale/return first so the lifecycle stays intact.','error');return;}
22076:   const ok=await showConfirm('Undo Sale '+saleNo+' return + relist?','This removes the Sale '+saleNo+' refund and its dependent relist for Sale '+(saleNo+1)+', then restores Sale '+saleNo+' as sold. Cashflow and lifetime P&L will recalculate from the corrected history.',{okLabel:'Undo both',danger:false});
22077:   if(!ok)return;
22078:   _dropReturnOwnedRelistMaintenance(i,r);_restoreCompletedSaleFromReturn(i,r);i.returnHistory.splice(rtnIdx,1);saveDB();renderItemPage(m,id);toast('Return + relist undone — Sale '+saleNo+' restored');
22079: }

22094: // Undo the most recent full return → move the item back to Sold. Prominent
22095: // counterpart to the timeline "✕ undo"; completes the per-state "go back".
22096: function undoLastReturn(m,id){
22097:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
22098:   if(!i||!i.returnHistory||!i.returnHistory.length)return;
22099:   let idx=-1;
22100:   for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
22101:     const r=i.returnHistory[k];
22102:     if((r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&!r._relistedAt){idx=k;break;}
22103:   }
22104:   if(idx<0)for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
22105:     const r=i.returnHistory[k];
22106:     if(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay'){idx=k;break;}
22107:   }
22108:   if(idx<0)for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){ if(i.returnHistory[k]){idx=k;break;} }
22109:   if(idx<0){toast('No return to undo');return;}
22110:   undoReturnEntry(m,id,idx);
22111: }

22113: // ── Unified "step back one state" ───────────────────────────────────────────
22114: // One consistent ← Back button on any state past the item's base. Dispatches to
22115: // the right existing revert. (v2.21.48)
22116: function _returnedToStockDate(i){
22117:   if(!i||!i.isReturned)return null;
22118:   const _fr=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
22119:   return (_fr&&_fr.loggedAt)?String(_fr.loggedAt).split('T')[0]:null;
22120: }
22121: function _latestRelistedReturnRef(i){
22122:   if(!i||!Array.isArray(i.returnHistory))return null;
22123:   for(let k=i.returnHistory.length-1;k>=0;k--){
22124:     const r=i.returnHistory[k];
22125:     if(r&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt)return {r:r,index:k};
22126:   }
22127:   return null;
22128: }
22129: function _stepBackTitle(i){
22130:   if(!i)return null;
22131:   if(i.scrappedAt)return 'Restore to stock';
22132:   if(i.isReturned)return 'Back to sold';
22133:   if(i.resaleSalePrice)return 'Undo Sale '+_currentResaleSaleNo(i);
22134:   if(i.dateSold)return 'Back to active stock';
22135:   // v1.4.6 — a returned item that has been relisted is NOT a first-cycle
22136:   // listing. Stepping back must undo that relist, never withdraw it to sourced.
22137:   const rr=_latestRelistedReturnRef(i);
22138:   if(_itemLifecycleState(i)==='listed'&&rr)return 'Undo relist';
22139:   if(i.state==='listed')return 'Back to sourced';
22140:   return null;
22141: }
22142: function stepBack(m,id){
22143:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
22144:   if(!i)return;
22145:   if(i.scrappedAt)return unScrap(m,id);
22146:   if(i.isReturned)return undoLastReturn(m,id);
22147:   if(i.resaleSalePrice)return undoResale(m,id);
22148:   if(i.dateSold)return revertToActive(m,id);
22149:   const rr=_latestRelistedReturnRef(i);
22150:   if(_itemLifecycleState(i)==='listed'&&rr)return undoRelist(m,id,rr.index);
22151:   if(i.state==='listed')return withdrawToSourced(m,id);
22152: }
22153: async function undoResale(m,id){
22154:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id;});
22155:   if(!i||!i.resaleSalePrice)return;
22156:   const saleNo=_currentResaleSaleNo(i);
22157:   const adjustments=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===saleNo;}).length;
22158:   const ok=await showConfirm(
22159:     'Undo Sale '+saleNo+'?',
22160:     'This clears Sale '+saleNo+' and returns the item to its active relisted stock.'+(adjustments?' '+adjustments+' Sale '+saleNo+' refund adjustment'+(adjustments===1?'':'s')+' will also be removed because that sale will no longer exist.':'')+' The relist fee remains because that listing was actually created.',
22161:     {okLabel:'Undo sale',danger:false}
22162:   );
22163:   if(!ok)return;
22164:   i.resaleSalePrice=null;i.resaleDateSold=null;i.resaleGrossProfit=null;
22165:   // Keep i.resalePlatform and the live i.shippingCost/postage/packaging/promo
22166:   // because they are the active relist settings. Completed-sale snapshots go.
22167:   i.resalePostage=null;i.resaleShippingCost=null;i.resalePackagingCost=null;i.resalePromoPercent=null;
22168:   if(adjustments)i.returnHistory=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)!==saleNo;});
22169:   i.isReturned=false;
22170:   i.state='listed';i.grossProfit=null;
22171:   saveDB();renderItemPage(m,id);toast('Sale '+saleNo+' undone — item back to listed');
22172: }

22185: async function revertToActive(m,id){
22186:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
22187:   if(!i)return;
22188:   // Safety: only revert a sold-but-not-returned, not-resold item.
22189:   if(!i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice||i.isReturned)return;
22190:   const s1Adjustments=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1;}).length;
22191:   const ok=await showConfirm(
22192:     'Move back to active?',
22193:     'This clears Sale 1 and returns the item to active stock.'+(s1Adjustments?' '+s1Adjustments+' Sale 1 refund adjustment'+(s1Adjustments===1?'':'s')+' will also be removed because that sale will no longer exist.':'')+' Use this only to correct a mis-marked sale.'
22194:   );
22195:   if(!ok)return;
22196:   i.dateSold=null;
22197:   if(s1Adjustments)i.returnHistory=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)!==1;});
22198:   i.state='listed';
22199:   i.grossProfit=null;
22200:   // Clear the sale-time platform — once back to active, the item must resolve
22201:   // its platform from defaultPlatform again (otherwise the item-page platform
22202:   // picker, which edits defaultPlatform, appears to do nothing).
22203:   i.soldOnPlatform=null;
22204:   saveDB();
22205:   toast('Moved back to active stock');
22206:   closePanel();
22207:   const cur=document.querySelector('.page.on')?document.querySelector('.page.on').id:null;
22208:   if(cur==='p-item')renderItemPage(m,id);
22209:   else if(cur==='p-monthly')renderMonth();
22210:   else if(cur==='p-stock')renderStock();
22211:   else renderSummary();
22212: }

22214: async function undoSale(m,id){
22215:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
22216:   if(!i)return;
22217:   const s1Adjustments=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1;}).length;
22218:   const ok=await showConfirm(
22219:     'Undo sale?',
22220:     'This removes Sale 1 and returns the item to Listed.'+(s1Adjustments?' '+s1Adjustments+' Sale 1 refund adjustment'+(s1Adjustments===1?'':'s')+' will also be removed because they cannot exist without the sale.':''),
22221:     {okLabel:'Undo sale',danger:false}
22222:   );
22223:   if(!ok)return;
22224:   i.dateSold=null;
22225:   i.isReturned=false;
22226:   i.state='listed';
22227:   i.grossProfit=null;
22228:   i.soldOnPlatform=null;
22229:   if(s1Adjustments)i.returnHistory=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)!==1;});
22230:   saveDB();
22231:   renderItemPage(m,id);
22232:   toast('Sale undone — item back to Listed');
22233: }

22235: function undoRelist(m,id,rtnIdx){
22236:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
22237:   if(!i||!i.returnHistory)return;
22238:   const r=i.returnHistory[rtnIdx];
22239:   if(!r||!r._relistedAt)return;
22240:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
22241: 
22242:   // A relist can only be undone while it is still the latest structural step.
22243:   // If the next Sale-N already completed, step that sale back first.
22244:   const _liveNo=i.resaleSalePrice?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):null;
22245:   const _laterFrozen=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r2){
22246:     return r2!==r&&(r2.type==='full_seller'||r2.type==='full_ebay')&&Math.max(1,Number(r2.saleNo)||1)>saleNo;
22247:   });
22248:   if((_liveNo&&_liveNo>saleNo)||_laterFrozen){
22249:     toast('Undo the later sale first, then undo this relist.','error');
22250:     return;
22251:   }
22252: 
22253:   // Remove relist markers from this return entry. The listing fee belongs to
22254:   // the relist action itself, so a correction that undoes the relist removes
22255:   // that fee from the event ledger as well.
22256:   const _undoneDate=r._relistedAt;
22257:   delete r._relistedAt;
22258:   delete r._relistCondition;
22259:   delete r._platformAtRelist;
22260:   delete r._listingFeeAtRelist;
22261: 
22262:   // v2.15.0 — drop the paired maintenance entry, or the relist counter and the
22263:   // freshness clock would keep counting a relist that has just been undone.
22264:   if(Array.isArray(i.refreshHistory)){
22265:     const _paired=i.refreshHistory
22266:       .map(function(e,ix){return {e:e,ix:ix};})
22267:       .filter(function(o){return o.e.type==='relist'&&o.e._fromReturn&&o.e.date===_undoneDate;})
22268:       .pop();
22269:     if(_paired)i.refreshHistory.splice(_paired.ix,1);
22270:     const _prior=i.refreshHistory.slice().reverse().find(function(e){return e.type==='relist';});
22271:     i._lastRelistAt=_prior?_prior.date:null;
22272:   }
22273: 
22274:   // confirmRelist clears the reusable resale slot after Sale 2+. Rebuild that
22275:   // failed completed sale from the immutable return snapshot so a subsequent
22276:   // "Undo return" can correctly move the item back to Sale N sold state.
22277:   if(saleNo>=2){
22278:     const _num=function(a,b,fallback){
22279:       if(a!=null)return Number(a)||0;
22280:       if(b!=null)return Number(b)||0;
22281:       return Number(fallback)||0;
22282:     };
22283:     i.resaleSalePrice=_num(r._salePriceAtReturn,r._salePriceAtRelist,0);
22284:     i.resaleDateSold=r._dateSoldAtReturn||null;
22285:     i.resalePostage=_num(r._postageAtReturn,r._postageAtRelist,0);
22286:     i.resaleShippingCost=_num(r._shippingCostAtReturn,r._shippingCostAtRelist,0);
22287:     i.resalePackagingCost=_num(r._packagingCostAtReturn,r._packagingCostAtRelist,0);
22288:     i.resalePromoPercent=_num(r._promoPercentAtReturn,r._promoPercentAtRelist,0);
22289:     i.resaleListingFee=_num(r._listingFeeAtReturn,null,0);
22290:     i.resalePlatform=r._platformAtReturn||i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
22291:     i.resaleBundleId=r._bundleIdAtReturn||null;
22292:     i.resaleBundleRef=r._bundleRefAtReturn||'';
22293:     i.resaleBundleTotal=Number(r._bundleTotalAtReturn)||0;
22294:     // Restore live fields used as defaults by the return/relist UI.
22295:     i.salePrice=i.resaleSalePrice;
22296:     i.postage=i.resalePostage;
22297:     i.shippingCost=i.resaleShippingCost;
22298:     i.packagingCost=i.resalePackagingCost;
22299:     i.promoPercent=i.resalePromoPercent;
22300:   }else{
22301:     // Sale 1 stays frozen in its original fields/snapshot; only remove the
22302:     // active resale-cycle fee that the now-undone relist had stamped.
22303:     i.resaleListingFee=0;
22304:   }
22305: 
22306:   // Restore returned state.
22307:   i.isReturned=true;
22308:   i.state='returned';
22309:   i.dateSold=null;
22310:   i.condition=undefined;
22311:   i.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(i);
22312:   saveDB();
22313:   renderItemPage(m,id);
22314:   toast('Relist undone — item back to Returned after Sale '+saleNo);
22315: }
22316:

22813: // Returns null for empty/invalid input. Used to bucket sale events by sale month
22814: // (rather than the listing-month bucket the item lives in inside DB).

22816: // Build a list of sale events that landed in month `m` (sale1 + sale2, attributed
22817: // by dateSold / resaleDateSold respectively). Each entry is a SALE EVENT — an item
22818: // with both an original sale and a resale in different months will produce two
22819: // entries, one per month.
22820: //
22821: // Returns array of: {item, month (DB key the item lives in), sale ('1' or '2'),
22822: //                    saleDate, salePrice, profit, roi, daysToSell, isReturned}
22823: //
22824: // `sale1` profit/revenue uses the item's original-sale-only state — for items
22825: // that have been relisted, this means reconstructing the original sale via the
22826: // snapshot stored on the full_return entry (see _origSalePrice convention).
22827: // `sale2` uses calcGrossProfit on the full item (which already includes the
22828: // relist accounting).
22829: // Return/refund events are dated independently from the original sale.
22830: // This preserves the original sale in its sale month and records the cash impact
22831: // in the month the return/refund was actually logged.
22832: 
22833: // ── F2/F3 event-profit reconciliation ──────────────────────────────────────
22834: // The sale-1, return and sale-2 events for one item must sum to
22835: // calcGrossProfit(i) — the canonical per-item all-time gross that feeds the
22836: // Dashboard, Money and cash. Historically the sale-2 event carried the WHOLE
22837: // item's gross (which already includes the sale-1 leg + the returns), so a
22838: // resold-after-return item double-counted; separately a fully-returned or
22839: // partially-refunded item's events missed the promoted-listing boost credit
22840: // and the partial-refund fee credit that calcGrossProfit applies. Fix: give
22841: // each event a BASE contribution (sale-1 = clean snapshot gross, return = its
22842: // own cash impact, sale-2 = 0) and route the whole residual onto the item's
22843: // TERMINAL event (sale-2 if resold, else the latest-dated return, else sale-1).
22844: // The set then sums to calcGrossProfit(i) by construction, for every shape.
22845: // DISPLAY-ONLY: the SA103/tax path (_buildPnLSummary → _saleBreakdown) reads
22846: // per-leg breakdowns and never reads these .profit fields.
22847: 
22848: 
22849: // v2.21.15 — The resale (sale 2) valued on its OWN leg: resale price with the
22850: // resale's shipping/packaging/promo and the item cost SUNK (0, paid in cycle 1).
22851: // Mirrors _sale1EventSnapshot. calcGrossProfit(this) == the item page's Sale 2
22852: // net, so the resale month shows honest resale economics.
22853: 
22854: // Fix B (B1): the Sales-History screen derives its month universe from item
22855: // STORAGE keys (purchase months). But events are attributed by SALE/RETURN
22856: // date, so any sale, resale or return dated in a month no purchase touches was
22857: // dropped from the all-time sum. This returns the set of 'MMM-YY' keys that
22858: // actually hold an event, so callers can widen their universe to cover them.

23310: function _recalcRelist(currentPL,salePrice,bpf,shipping,packaging,postage){
23311:   const promoInput=parseFloat(document.getElementById('rl-promo-input').value)||0;
23312:   const grossInc=salePrice-bpf;
23313:   const promoCost=_calcPromoFee('ebay',salePrice,postage,promoInput/100);
23314:   const rev=salePrice+postage-bpf;
23315:   const cost=shipping+packaging+promoCost;
23316:   const rlProfit=+(rev-cost).toFixed(2);
23317:   const combinedP=+(currentPL+rlProfit).toFixed(2);
23318:   const promoEl=document.getElementById('rl-promo-cost');
23319:   const profitEl=document.getElementById('rl-profit');
23320:   const lifetimeEl=document.getElementById('rl-lifetime');
23321:   if(promoEl)promoEl.textContent='−'+fmt(promoCost);

23470: function ipSave(m,id,field,val){
23471:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
23472:   if(!i)return;
23473:   i[field]=val;
23474:   // Recalc profit when sale date changes
23475:   if(field==='dateSold'){
23476:     if(val){i.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(i);}
23477:     else{i.grossProfit=null;}
23478:   }
23479:   // Category change on a SOLD item must re-store grossProfit, not just refresh
23480:   // the display — eBay Biz FVF is category-rated, so the stored profit (used in
23481:   // Summary totals, monthly P&L and tax) would otherwise stay stale.
23482:   if(field==='category'&&i.dateSold){
23483:     i.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(i);
23484:   }
23485:   saveDB();
23486:   // Re-render item page for fields that affect the P&L display.
23487:   // category matters because eBay Biz FVF is category-rated — without this the
23488:   // fee label, BPF and Net Profit wouldn't refresh until the next full re-render.
23489:   if(field==='dateSold'||field==='dateListed'||field==='category'){
23490:     const pg=document.querySelector('.page.on');
23491:     if(pg&&pg.id==='p-item')renderItemPage(m,id);
23492:   }
23493: }

26210: /* ========================================================================
26211:  * RETRADE-UK v1.4.9 — AUTHORITATIVE CLOUD + LIFECYCLE EVENT DURABILITY
26212:  * 2026-09-01
26213:  *
26214:  * Fixes the live regression reproduced with:
26215:  * Sold -> Return 1 -> Relist -> Sold 2 -> Return 2.
26216:  *
26217:  * Key rules:
26218:  *  - a large stale recovery queue is quarantined, never blindly replayed;
26219:  *  - normal persistence does NOT reload/rebase the whole cloud before every
26220:  *    local write (v1.4.8's loop could re-apply hundreds of old snapshots);
26221:  *  - item-return rows are stable event records: existing ids are updated,
26222:  *    new events append, and unrelated remote history is not implicitly deleted;
26223:  *  - explicit user undo is the only path that tombstones return-event rows;
26224:  *  - stale item recovery merges against its saved base where possible; a
26225:  *    legacy stale snapshot with no trustworthy base is quarantined instead of
26226:  *    silently overwriting a newer cloud lifecycle.
26227:  * ======================================================================== */

26233:   var RETURN_CONFLICT_BASE='retrade_return_conflicts_v149';

501: function _monthKeyFromDate(ds){
502:   if(!ds)return null;
503:   // Tolerate both 'YYYY-MM-DD' (ISO) and dates with time. Avoid Date() parse drift.
504:   const parts=String(ds).slice(0,10).split('-');
505:   if(parts.length<3)return null;
506:   const yr=parseInt(parts[0],10);
507:   const mo=parseInt(parts[1],10);
508:   if(!yr||!mo||mo<1||mo>12)return null;
509:   return MONTHS[mo-1]+'-'+String(yr).slice(-2);
510: }

1134: function calcEbayBPF(salePrice){
1135:   if(!salePrice||salePrice<=0)return 0;
1136:   const feeOnNet=function(net){
1137:     let f=0.10;
1138:     f+=0.07*Math.min(net,20);
1139:     if(net>20)f+=0.04*Math.min(net-20,280);
1140:     if(net>300)f+=0.02*Math.min(net-300,3700);
1141:     // eBay UK private Buyer Protection is capped at the £4,000 band.
1142:     // Any portion of the seller's item price above £4,000 adds no further BPF.
1143:     return f;
1144:   }
1145:   let net=salePrice;
1146:   for(let j=0;j<10;j++)net=salePrice-feeOnNet(net);
1147:   return+feeOnNet(net).toFixed(2);
1148: }

1301: function _calcPromoFee(platformId,itemPrice,buyerPostage,promoPercent){

1305:   const post=Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);

1313: function _sellerRefundCreditRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1314:   const entries=(item&&item.returnHistory)||[];
1315:   const target=Math.max(1,Number(saleNo)||1);
1316:   // A seller-handled FULL refund represents a complete reversal for fee-credit
1317:   // purposes. eBay-stepped-in refunds deliberately do not qualify.
1318:   if(entries.some(function(r){
1319:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1320:     return rs===target&&r.type==='full_seller';
1321:   }))return 1;
1322:   const refunded=entries.filter(function(r){
1323:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1324:     return rs===target&&r.type==='partial_seller';
1325:   }).reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+(Number(r.refundAmount)||0);},0);
1326:   const total=Math.max(0,Number(salePrice)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);
1327:   if(total<=0||refunded<=0)return 0;
1328:   return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,refunded/total));

1350: function _netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,platformId,salePrice,buyerPostage){

1356:     // processed. Partial refunds therefore receive the proportional share.

1361:   // it only because private-eBay salePrice is stored as the buyer-facing item

1412: function _privateEbayBuyerFeeRefund(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1413:   const bpf=calcEbayBPF(salePrice);
1414:   if(!bpf)return 0;
1415:   const target=Math.max(1,Number(saleNo)||1);
1416:   const refunded=((item&&item.returnHistory)||[]).filter(function(r){
1417:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1418:     return rs===target&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay'||r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');
1419:   }).reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+(Number(r.refundAmount)||0);},0);
1420:   if(refunded<=0||salePrice<=0)return 0;
1421:   // eBay says the private Buyer Protection fee is refunded pro-rata with the
1422:   // item-price refund. It is eBay's money, not a seller cost. RETRADE's return
1423:   // form historically stores the buyer-facing refund amount, so remove the
1424:   // BPF share before treating that refund as seller cash outflow.
1425:   const ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,refunded/Math.max(0.01,Number(salePrice)||0)));
1426:   return +(bpf*ratio).toFixed(2);

1433: function calcItemRevenue(i){
1434:   if(!i.salePrice)return 0;
1435:   // Returned and not yet resold — revenue = 0
1436:   if(i.isReturned)return 0;
1437:   // Returned then resold — only the resale price counts as revenue
1438:   const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(r=>r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
1439:   const _wasReturnedThenResold=i.resaleSalePrice&&_hadFullReturn;
1440:   if(_wasReturnedThenResold)return+(i.resaleSalePrice||0).toFixed(2);
1441:   // BUGFIX (Session E1): fully-returned-then-relisted-but-not-yet-resold —
1442:   // original sale was reversed; sale 2 hasn't happened. Revenue = 0.
1443:   if(_hadFullReturn&&!i.resaleSalePrice)return 0;
1444:   // Normal sold (including partial refunds — partial doesn't reverse the sale).
1445:   // F9 guard: resaleSalePrice only ever counts via the returned-then-resold path
1446:   // above; a non-returned item's revenue is its single sale price. Returning
1447:   // salePrice alone prevents a double-count if resaleSalePrice is ever set here
1448:   // without a full return (a latent/corrupt state).
1449:   return+(i.salePrice||0).toFixed(2);
1450: }
