1452: function calcGrossProfit(i){
1453:   if(!i.salePrice)return null;
1454:   // v1.3 — once an item has a real completed sale, realised item P&L is the
1455:   // chronological sum of immutable Sale-N and return events. This removes the
1456:   // old Sale-1/Sale-2 ceiling and stops a later relist from rewriting an older
1457:   // cycle. _saleBreakdown includes partner share for statement purposes, so add
1458:   // it back here: calcGrossProfit remains PRE-partner and calcNetProfit owns the
1459:   // single partner deduction contract.
1460:   const _actualCycles=(typeof _saleCycleNumbers==='function')?_saleCycleNumbers(i):[];
1461:   if(_actualCycles.length){
1462:     let _actualGross=0;
1463:     _actualCycles.forEach(function(n){
1464:       const cyc=_saleCycleSnapshot(i,n);if(!cyc)return;
1465:       const b=_saleBreakdown({item:i,sale:String(n),saleDate:cyc.date,salePrice:cyc.price,snapshot:cyc,isReturnAdjustment:false,isReturned:false});
1466:       _actualGross+=(Number(b.netProfit)||0)+(Number(b.partnerSplit)||0);
1467:     });
1468:     (i.returnHistory||[]).forEach(function(r){
1469:       const b=_saleBreakdown({item:i,sale:'R',saleDate:_returnEventDate(r),isReturnAdjustment:true,isReturned:true,returnEntry:r});
1470:       _actualGross+=(Number(b.netProfit)||0);
1471:     });
1472:     // A relisted-but-not-yet-resold item has incurred the NEXT insertion fee,
1473:     // but no Sale-N event exists yet to carry it. Recognise that sunk fee once.
1474:     if(!i.isReturned&&!i.dateSold&&!i.resaleSalePrice){
1475:       const pending=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt;});
1476:       if(pending&&pending._listingFeeAtRelist!=null)_actualGross-=Math.max(0,Number(pending._listingFeeAtRelist)||0);
1477:     }
1478:     return +_actualGross.toFixed(2);
1479:   }
1480:   // F4 — a sale marked fully returned with NO return record (empty returnHistory)
1481:   // and no resale has zero revenue; without an entry to reverse it the code below
1482:   // reports phantom profit. Force 0 to match calcItemRevenue's isReturned rule.
1483:   if(i.isReturned && !i.resaleSalePrice && !(i.returnHistory||[]).length)return 0;
1484:   // ── Multi-cycle full returns ────────────────────────────────────────────
1485:   // When the item is physically back in stock after a full return, acquisition
1486:   // cost/parts remain recoverable stock. Lifetime realised P&L is therefore the
1487:   // sunk selling cost of each failed completed sale cycle. Sale 2+ cycles are
1488:   // frozen on their return rows before the live resale slot is reused.
1489:   const _fullRets=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
1490:   if(i.isReturned&&_fullRets.length){
1491:     let failed=0;
1492:     _fullRets.forEach(function(r){failed+=_failedCycleLoss(i,r);});
1493:     const listing=(Number(i.listingFee)||0)+(Number(i.resaleListingFee)||0);
1494:     return +(-(failed+listing)).toFixed(2);
1495:   }
1496:   // Partial refund handling for fees:
1497:   //   partial_seller (seller agreed): eBay refunds the fee difference — recalc BPF/promo on net sale price
1498:   //   partial_ebay (eBay stepped in): eBay keeps the original fee — BPF/promo stay on full sale price
1499:   //   Full returns are handled separately below (fee fully refunded on relist path)
1500:   // v2.19.31 — Sale-1 partial refunds only. saleNo===2 belongs to the resale
1501:   // branch below; untagged (legacy) entries are Sale 1 by default.
1502:   const partialSellerRefund=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_seller'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1)
1503:     .reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1504:   // E3 P0 #1 — Resolve sale 1 economics. For items that were relisted then
1505:   // resold, confirmRelist overwrites i.shippingCost/postage/packaging/promo
1506:   // with sale 2 values. The pre-relist snapshot is stored on the latest full-
1507:   // return entry (_*AtRelist fields). When present, prefer the snapshot for
1508:   // sale 1 cost/fee calculations. Older items without snapshot fall back to
1509:   // current values (slightly wrong but no worse than before).
1510:   const _hasResale=!!i.resaleSalePrice;
1511:   const _fullReturnEntry=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(r=>(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1);
1512:   const _snap=(_hasResale&&_fullReturnEntry)?_fullReturnEntry:null;
1513:   const _s1SalePrice=_snap?(_fullReturnEntry._salePriceAtRelist||_fullReturnEntry._salePriceAtReturn||i.salePrice||0):(i.salePrice||0);
1514:   const _s1Postage=(_snap&&_fullReturnEntry._postageAtRelist!==undefined)?_fullReturnEntry._postageAtRelist:(i.postage||0);
1515:   const _s1Shipping=(_snap&&_fullReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist!==undefined)?_fullReturnEntry._shippingCostAtRelist:(i.shippingCost||0);
1516:   const _s1Packaging=(_snap&&_fullReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist!==undefined)?_fullReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist:(i.packagingCost||0);
1517:   const _s1Promo=(_snap&&_fullReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist!==undefined)?_fullReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist:(i.promoPercent||0);
1518:   const _baseSale=_snap?_s1SalePrice:i.salePrice;
1519:   const _s1PlatformId=_s1Platform(i);
1520:   // Canonical fee path: keep the ORIGINAL sale base intact, then apply any
1521:   // eligible refund credit to the fee itself. This is essential for banded
1522:   // eBay Business categories: recalculating a fictitious lower sale can move
1523:   // money between bands and produce the wrong credit.
1524:   const bpf=_netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(i,1,_s1PlatformId,_baseSale,_s1Postage);
1525:   // Advertising uses the same refund-credit rules as the platform fee. A seller-
1526:   // handled eligible refund can credit some/all of the ad charge; an eBay-
1527:   // stepped-in refund keeps the original charge. Never special-case "full" to 0.
1528:   const promoCost=_netPromoFeeAfterRefunds(i,1,_s1PlatformId,_baseSale,_s1Postage,_s1Promo);
1529:   // Determine if this item has been relisted after a full return
1530:   // (isReturned=false but returnHistory has a full return entry, and no dateSold / resaleSalePrice)
1531:   // Full returns only: eBay refunds BPF back to seller (transaction fully reversed)
1532:   // Partial returns: fee treatment depends on refund type (see above)
1533:   const _wasRelisted = !i.isReturned && !i.dateSold && !i.resaleSalePrice &&
1534:     (i.returnHistory||[]).some(r=>r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
1535:   // Refund principal is a cash outflow regardless of who handled it. Fee relief
1536:   // is modelled separately above, so revenue is never distorted by changing the
1537:   // original transaction base.
1538:   const partialEbayRefund=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_ebay'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1)
1539:     .reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1540:   // Private eBay's stored sale/refund amounts are buyer-facing and include the
1541:   // Buyer Protection component. eBay returns that component itself, so add its
1542:   // prorated reversal back before treating a partial refund as seller cash out.
1543:   const _s1PrivateBpfRefund=(_s1PlatformId==='ebay')?(i.returnHistory||[])
1544:     .filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===1&&(r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');})
1545:     .reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+_refundCreditsForEntry(i,r).platformCredit;},0):0;
1546:   // When a full-returned item has been relisted, its sale revenue is gone. Any
1547:   // seller fee that was NOT credited remains a real loss. Private-eBay BPF is
1548:   // buyer-funded rather than a seller fee, so it must not become a phantom cost.
1549:   const _platformFeeIsSellerCost=(_s1PlatformId!=='ebay');
1550:   const rev=_wasRelisted
1551:     ? (_platformFeeIsSellerCost?-bpf:0)
1552:     : _baseSale+_s1Postage-bpf-partialSellerRefund-partialEbayRefund+_s1PrivateBpfRefund;
1553:   const returnCost=(i.returnHistory||[]).reduce(function(s,r){
1554:     const fullReturn=(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
1555:     const _rSale=(r.saleNo===2)?2:1;
1556:     if(fullReturn&&_rSale===1){
1557:       if(_wasRelisted){
1558:         // Sale principal already removed above; only return postage remains here.
1559:         return s+(r.returnPostage||0);
1560:       }
1561:       const refund=Math.max(0,Number(r.refundAmount)||0);
1562:       if(_s1PlatformId==='ebay'){
1563:         // Private eBay: the refund field is buyer-facing. eBay refunds its own
1564:         // Buyer Protection share, so only the seller-funded portion is cash out.
1565:         const buyerFeeRefund=_privateEbayBuyerFeeRefund(i,1,_baseSale,_s1Postage);
1566:         return s+Math.max(0,refund-buyerFeeRefund)+(r.returnPostage||0);
1567:       }
1568:       // Seller-fee platforms: fee credits are already reflected by `bpf` above;
1569:       // the refund principal itself is still paid back in full.
1570:       return s+refund+(r.returnPostage||0);
1571:     }
1572:     // Partial refunds: refund principal handled via revenue adjustment above.
1573:     // Only charge return postage here (refund amount itself is already netted out).
1574:     return s+(r.returnPostage||0);
1575:   },0);
1576:   const partsCost=calcPartsCost(i);
1577:   // Vinted (and any future buyer-pays-postage-only platform): seller pays nothing to ship
1578:   const _platForCost=_itemPlatform(i);
1579:   const _sellerPaysShipping=(_platForCost!=='vinted'&&_platForCost!=='fb');
1580:   // v2.19.26 — both listings' insertion fees. resaleListingFee is added here
1581:   // rather than inside the resale branch below so a relisted-but-unsold item
1582:   // still carries the fee it has already been charged.
1583:   const listingFee=(i.listingFee||0)+(i.resaleListingFee||0);
1584:   // v2.21.45 — a fully-returned item that hasn't been resold is BACK IN STOCK: its cost
1585:   // (buy + parts) is recoverable capital, not a realised loss. Only the sunk selling costs
1586:   // (shipping/pkg/promo/return/fees/listing) count. Resold items keep the cost (consumed by
1587:   // the resale, v2.21.16). Display-only — calcGrossProfit does NOT feed SA103.
1588:   const _backInStock=!!i.isReturned&&(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
1589:   const cost=(_backInStock?0:(i.costPrice||0))+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Shipping:0)+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Packaging:0)+promoCost+returnCost+(_backInStock?0:partsCost)+listingFee;
1590:   let profit=rev-cost;
1591:   // Second sale (resold after return)
1592:   if(i.resaleSalePrice){
1593:     const rPostage=i.resalePostage!=null?i.resalePostage:(i.postage||0);
1594:     const _rPlatformId=i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
1595:     const _liveResaleNo=_currentResaleCycleNo(i);
1596:     const rBPF=_netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage);
1597:     const rPromoCost=_netPromoFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage,i.resalePromoPercent||0);
1598:     // v2.19.31 — partial refunds tagged to Sale 2. Principal is deducted here;
1599:     // any eligible fee/boost credit is already reflected in the canonical net
1600:     // fee helpers above.
1601:     const rPartialSeller=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_seller'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1602:     const rPartialEbay=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_ebay'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1603:     const _rPrivateBpfRefund=(_rPlatformId==='ebay')?(i.returnHistory||[])
1604:       .filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo&&(r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');})
1605:       .reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+_refundCreditsForEntry(i,r).platformCredit;},0):0;
1606:     const _rFull=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');});
1607:     let _rFullSellerCash=0;
1608:     if(_rFull){
1609:       _rFullSellerCash=Math.max(0,Number(_rFull.refundAmount)||0);
1610:       if(_rPlatformId==='ebay')_rFullSellerCash=Math.max(0,_rFullSellerCash-_refundCreditsForEntry(i,_rFull).platformCredit);
1611:     }
1612:     const rRev=i.resaleSalePrice+rPostage-rBPF-(i.resaleShippingCost||0)-(i.resalePackagingCost||0)-rPromoCost
1613:       -rPartialSeller-rPartialEbay+_rPrivateBpfRefund-_rFullSellerCash;
1614:     profit+=rRev;
1615:   }
1616:   return+profit.toFixed(2);
1617: }
1618: 
1619: function calcNetProfit(i){
1620:   const gross=calcGrossProfit(i);
1621:   if(gross===null)return null;
1622:   const owed=_accountItemOwed(i); // null if no split set, or item unsold
1623:   if(owed===null)return gross;
1624:   return+(gross-owed).toFixed(2);
1625: }

1973: function calcCOGSOnly(i){
1974:   return +((i.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(i)).toFixed(2);
1975: }

1977: function _estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(i,profit){
1978:   if(i==null||profit==null)return 0;
1979:   if(_itemAccountType(i)==='supplier')return 0;
1980:   if(i.accountPaidAmount!=null)return +Math.max(0,Number(i.accountPaidAmount)||0).toFixed(2);
1981:   let pct=i.accountSplitPercent;
1982:   if(pct==null&&i.accountId){
1983:     const acct=(_accounts||[]).find(function(a){return a.id===i.accountId;});
1984:     if(acct&&acct.defaultSplitPercent!=null)pct=acct.defaultSplitPercent;
1985:   }
1986:   if(pct==null)return 0;
1987:   pct=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(pct)||0));
1988:   return +(Math.max(0,Number(profit)||0)*(pct/100)).toFixed(2);
1989: }

1991: // v1.4.10 — Expected profit is an operational stock metric, not merely the
1992: // current realised P&L. If a physical item is back in hand after one or more
1993: // full-return cycles, show what its LIFETIME P&L would become if the next sale
1994: // completes at the current expected ask. Acquisition cost stays sunk/recoverable
1995: // exactly once; the hypothetical next cycle only adds its incremental economics.
1996: function calcEstGrossProfit(i){
1997:   if(!i)return null;
1998:   const onHand=!i.scrappedAt&&(!!i.isReturned||(!i.dateSold&&!i.resaleSalePrice));
1999:   const hasCompletedReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');});
2000: 
2001:   if(onHand&&hasCompletedReturn){
2002:     const base=calcGrossProfit(i);
2003:     if(base===null)return null;
2004:     const ask=(Number(i.estSalePrice)>0)?Number(i.estSalePrice):Math.max(0,Number(i.salePrice)||0);
2005:     if(ask<=0)return base;
2006:     const platformId=_itemPlatform(i);
2007:     const post=Math.max(0,Number(i.postage)||0);
2008:     const sellerPostage=(typeof _sellerPostageIncome==='function')?_sellerPostageIncome(platformId,post):post;
2009:     const bpf=Math.max(0,Number(calcPlatformBPF(i,ask,post,platformId))||0);
2010:     const promo=Math.max(0,Number(_calcPromoFee(platformId,ask,post,Number(i.promoPercent)||0))||0);
2011:     const sellerPays=(typeof _sellerPaysOutbound==='function')?_sellerPaysOutbound(platformId):(platformId!=='vinted'&&platformId!=='fb');
2012:     const fulfilment=sellerPays?(Math.max(0,Number(i.shippingCost)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(i.packagingCost)||0)):0;
2013:     // Any relist insertion fee already recorded on the return event is already
2014:     // contained in calcGrossProfit(base), so never deduct it twice here.
2015:     const nextCycle=ask+sellerPostage-bpf-promo-fulfilment;
2016:     return +(base+nextCycle).toFixed(2);
2017:   }
2018: 
2019:   // Fresh listed stock keeps the established estimate semantics.
2020:   if(!i.salePrice)return null;
2021:   const base=calcGrossProfit(i);
2022:   if(base===null)return null;
2023:   return +base.toFixed(2);
2024: }
2025: 
2026: function calcEstProfit(i){
2027:   const gross=calcEstGrossProfit(i);
2028:   if(gross===null)return null;
2029:   return +(gross-_estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(i,gross)).toFixed(2);
2030: }

2032: function calcROI(i){
2033:   if(!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt)return null;
2034:   // ROI denominator = capital actually outlaid to acquire and prep the item.
2035:   // eBay fees (BPF) and promo fees are deductions from revenue, not investments —
2036:   // they do not belong in the denominator. This keeps ROI honest:
2037:   //   e.g. £15 cost → £39 net profit = 260% ROI (not 135%).
2038:   let totalInvested=(i.costPrice||0)+(i.shippingCost||0)+(i.packagingCost||0)+calcPartsCost(i);
2039:   // For resold items: add resale shipping/packaging (real outlay on the second go).
2040:   if(i.resaleSalePrice){
2041:     totalInvested+=(i.resaleShippingCost||0)+(i.resalePackagingCost||0);
2042:   }
2043:   if(totalInvested<=0)return null;
2044:   return+((calcNetProfit(i)/totalInvested)*100).toFixed(1);
2045: }
2046: 
2047: function calcMargin(i){
2048:   if(!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt)return null;
2049:   const rev=calcItemRevenue(i);
2050:   if(!rev||rev<=0)return null;
2051:   const profit=calcNetProfit(i);
2052:   if(profit===null)return null;
2053:   return+((profit/rev)*100).toFixed(1);
2054: }

2056: function daysBetween(d1,d2){
2057:   // E3 P2 #10 — Null/empty inputs previously yielded ~20,000 days
2058:   // (Unix epoch fallthrough), bucketing null-dateListed items into
2059:   // 'stale'. Return -1 sentinel; filter call sites should guard on
2060:   // i.dateListed before calling rather than relying on the sentinel,
2061:   // because no single sentinel value can be excluded from all bucket
2062:   // ranges simultaneously (fresh=<=30 includes -1, stale=>=90 doesn't).
2063:   if(!d1||!d2)return -1;
2064:   const a=new Date(d1);
2065:   const b=new Date(d2);
2066:   if(isNaN(a)||isNaN(b))return -1;
2067:   const diff=Math.abs(b-a);
2068:   return Math.floor(diff/(1000*60*60*24));
2069: }

2071: function _effectiveListDate(i){
2072:   if(!i)return null;
2073:   if(!i.dateSold&&!i.resaleSalePrice){
2074:     // Most recent relist wins. confirmRelist and markRelisted both maintain
2075:     // _lastRelistAt, so this is the authoritative clock-reset point.
2076:     if(i._lastRelistAt)return i._lastRelistAt;
2077:     // v2.15.0 legacy fallback — items relisted BEFORE this version have a
2078:     // _relistedAt on their return entry but no maintenance record. The old
2079:     // guard here was `i.isReturned && !i.resaleSalePrice`, which could never
2080:     // be true for a relisted item because confirmRelist sets isReturned=false
2081:     // when it restores the item to active stock. The branch was therefore dead
2082:     // and those items silently kept their ORIGINAL dateListed age.
2083:     const r=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt;});
2084:     if(r&&r._relistedAt)return r._relistedAt;
2085:     // v2.21.48 — returned-and-back item is fresh again; clock from the return date.
2086:     const _rd=(typeof _returnedToStockDate==='function')?_returnedToStockDate(i):null;
2087:     if(_rd)return _rd;
2088:   }
2089:   return i.dateListed||null;
2090: }

2092: function _lifetimeListDays(i){
2093:   if(!i||!i.dateListed)return null;
2094:   const end=i.dateSold||i.resaleDateSold||new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
2095:   return daysBetween(i.dateListed,end);
2096: }

2098: function _timeToList(i){
2099:   if(!i || !i.dateSourced || !i.dateListed) return null;
2100:   const d=daysBetween(i.dateSourced,i.dateListed);
2101:   return d<0 ? null : d;
2102: }
